local previewFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Builds") or Instance.new("Folder", workspace)
previewFolder.Name = "Builds"

for _, v in pairs(partTable) do
    local itemFolder = game.ReplicatedStorage.ClientItemInfo:FindFirstChild(v.Name)
    if not itemFolder then continue end
    local model = itemFolder:FindFirstChildOfClass("Model")
    if not model then continue end
    local part = model:Clone()
    part.Parent = previewFolder
    part:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(v.CFrame)
    part.Name = v.Name
    
    if v.TreeValue then
        local tv = Instance.new("StringValue")
        tv.Name  = "TreeValue"
        tv.Value = v.TreeValue
        tv.Parent = part
        
        local wood = part:FindFirstChild("BuildDependentWood", true)
        if wood then
            local WC = {
                Generic    = Color3.fromRGB(204,142,105), Oak       = Color3.fromRGB(234,184,146),
                Cherry     = Color3.fromRGB(163, 75, 75), Fir       = Color3.fromRGB(215,197,154),
                Birch      = Color3.fromRGB(205,205,205), Walnut    = Color3.fromRGB(105, 64, 40),
                Koa        = Color3.fromRGB(143, 76, 42), Volcano   = Color3.fromRGB(255,  0,  0),
                GreenSwampy= Color3.fromRGB( 52,142, 64), GoldSwampy= Color3.fromRGB(226,155, 64),
                Palm       = Color3.fromRGB(226,220,188), SnowGlow  = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,  0),
                Frost      = Color3.fromRGB(159,243,233), CaveCrawler=Color3.fromRGB( 16, 42,220),
                BlueSpruce = Color3.fromRGB(159,173,192), LoneCave  = Color3.fromRGB(248,248,248),
                Spooky     = Color3.fromRGB(170, 85,  0), SpookyNeon= Color3.fromRGB(170, 85,  0),
            }
            if WC[v.TreeValue] then wood.Color = WC[v.TreeValue] end
            if v.TreeValue == "Spooky"     then wood.Material = Enum.Material.Granite end
            if v.TreeValue == "SpookyNeon" then wood.Material = Enum.Material.Neon    end
        end
    end
    
    for _, obj in pairs(part:GetChildren()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Transparency == 0 then obj.Transparency = 0.3 end
    end
end
